{"type":"api-request-blob","sessionId":"89c66567-64ca-4ceb-848f-1412ad049d55","hash":"838d9533e13319e5945dc7adade9e74c0788fd2aec52051633ed1b8666a4d019","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011YQLszK4E482kFSY8WmC4Q","type":"tool_result","content":"All of the code in this project was written by Claude Vode using the pipeline of grill, spec, tickets, and  `/implement`, `/tdd`, `/code-review` and `/pr` for each ticket. Except for part of the session 01 using Sonnect 5.5 medium effort, all of the rest was done by Opus 5.5 high effort. I made the decisions in the grill, read all the review, and either confirmed or rejected Claude�M-^@M-^Ys suggestions before anything was merged. I worked in two languages, because the project is in English, sometimes when the feedback is long and I don�M-^@M-^Yt fully understand what it was saying, I will send it to a different session to let it explain to me to make sure I understand the context, then I will work with it and send my answer back. It also helped me collected the evidences and resources for my report and this statement, and based on the information, I wrote the report and this statement in my own word.$\n$\nBelow are the two prompts that I sent$\n$\n1. On the budget plan (session 12): \"If I hit the 5 hour limit, I'll just upgrade my plan, because I have other projects to work with Claude code, such as the project for IPHS 484 and I notice that opus 5.5 can de better job than sonnet 5, so it is necessary to stay in opus 5.5.\"$\n2. On one-character Chinese nicknames in T07 (session 16): \"single character nickname in Chinese is rare but some people would use, so I think it is better (1) When an Admin records a one character Chinese nickname, show a notice on the term page that it will match every occurrence of that character and may cause many warnings. The Admin can still save it. (2) In the preview, show each unmarked nickname as one warning with a count, instead of one warning per occurrence.\"$\n$\n$\nOne of the model failure is that in T08 (#9, session 17), Claude skipped the test-first step of `/tdd`. Its own summary said \"most Tournament tests passed the first time they ran, because I wrote that code in one go.\" The tests was passed, and `/code-review` only reads the diff, so it would not been noticed, but Claude reported it to me and It then broke the recap link on purpose to prove the matching tests could fail, and I accepted that repair but decided to mention it later in the report or AI statment because I think it is a perfect example. $\n=== README head\n# 大巴扎攻略站 (The Bazaar Guides)\n\nA small CMS for the Chinese-speaking community of the card game *The Bazaar*.\nA few streamers (up主) run the site as Admins; invited writers join as Editors\nand write Guides, which an Admin reviews and publishes. Readers, who have no\naccount, find a written build for a given Hero and Patch on the public site\ninstead of scrubbing through videos.\n\nThe admin console runs on a laptop only. `cms publish` renders the published\ncontent into `site/` as static HTML in Chinese and English, and that is what\nGitHub Pages serves. The project's vocabulary is in `CONTEXT.md`.\n\n## Live URL\n\nhttps://avulpix0412.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\n\n## Run locally\n\nNeeds [uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/) and Python 3.12 or newer.\n\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/Avulpix0412/iphs400-mp2-cms.git\ncd iphs400-mp2-cms\nuv sync\ncp .env.example .env    # then change the passwords and the secret key\nuv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\nuv run cms serve    # then open http://localhost:8000/login\n```\n\nRun these from the repository root: the `cms` command and the seed script load\n`.env` from the folder you run them in. A variable already set in your shell\nwins over the same key in `.env`, and `uv run --env-file .env …` still works.\nThe loader skips blank lines, `#` comments and a leading `export `, strips the\nquotes around a quoted value, and warns about (and skips) any line that is not\n`KEY=value`.\n\nThe seed script creates `admin@example.test` (Admin, 演示管理员) and\n`editor@example.test` (Editor, 演示编辑), with the passwords you set in `.env` as\n`CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`. It refuses to run without them;\nthere are no default passwords. It also adds demo Heroes, the Patches 6.5–6.7\n(6.7 is the Current Patch), Draft, Submitted and Published Guides, an About\npage and two Tournament items. Running it again leaves existing records unchanged.\n\nTo build the public site from what is published:\n\n```bash\nuv run cms publish    # writes site/; it prints a preview command\n```\n\n0dcdb89 #31: the seed script and the cms command load .env themselves\nc13f336 docs: rewrite README for 大巴扎攻略站 and set the demo site title\nde9389d T02: Admins manage Heroes and Patches\n32b4bd3 T01: Admins and Editors sign in and out\n4455080 Initial commit","is_error":false}]}}

It also helped me collected the evidences and resources for my report and this statement, and based on the information, I wrote the report and this statement in my own word.$\n$\nBelow are the two prompts that I sent$\n$\n1. On the budget plan (session 12): \"If I hit the 5 hour limit, I'll just upgrade my plan, because I have other projects to work with Claude code, such as the project for IPHS 484 and I notice that opus 5.5 can de better job than sonnet 5, so it is necessary to stay in opus 5.5.\"$\n2. On one-character Chinese nicknames in T07 (session 16): \"single character nickname in Chinese is rare but some people would use, so I think it is better (1) When an Admin records a one character Chinese nickname, show a notice on the term page that it will match every occurrence of that character and may cause many warnings. The Admin can still save it. (2) In the preview, show each unmarked nickname as one warning with a count, instead of one warning per occurrence.\"$\n$\n$\nOne of the model failure is that in T08 (#9, session 17), Claude skipped the test-first step of `/tdd`. Its own summary said \"most Tournament tests passed the first time they ran, because I wrote that code in one go.\" The tests was passed, and `/code-review` only reads the diff, so it would not been noticed, but Claude reported it to me and It then broke the recap link on purpose to prove the matching tests could fail, and I accepted that repair but decided to mention it later in the report or AI statment because I think it is a perfect example. $\n=== README head\n# 大巴扎攻略站 (The Bazaar Guides)\n\nA small CMS for the Chinese-speaking community of the card game *The Bazaar*.\nA few streamers (up主) run the site as Admins; invited writers join as Editors\nand write Guides, which an Admin reviews and publishes. Readers, who have no\naccount, find a written build for a given Hero and Patch on the public site\ninstead of scrubbing through videos.\n\nThe admin console runs on a laptop only. `cms publish` renders the published\ncontent into `site/` as static HTML in Chinese and English, and that is what\nGitHub Pages serves. The project's vocabulary is in `CONTEXT.md`.\n\n## Live URL\n\nhttps://avulpix0412.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\n\n## Run locally\n\nNeeds [uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/) and Python 3.12 or newer.\n\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/Avulpix0412/iphs400-mp2-cms.git\ncd iphs400-mp2-cms\nuv sync\ncp .env.example .env    # then change the passwords and the secret key\nuv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\nuv run cms serve    # then open http://localhost:8000/login\n```\n\nRun these from the repository root: the `cms` command and the seed script load\n`.env` from the folder you run them in. A variable already set in your shell\nwins over the same key in `.env`, and `uv run --env-file .env …` still works.\nThe loader skips blank lines, `#` comments and a leading `export `, strips the\nquotes around a quoted value, and warns about (and skips) any line that is not\n`KEY=value`.\n\nThe seed script creates `admin@example.test` (Admin, 演示管理员) and\n`editor@example.test` (Editor, 演示编辑), with the passwords you set in `.env` as\n`CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`. It refuses to run without them;\nthere are no default passwords. It also adds demo Heroes, the Patches 6.5–6.7\n(6.7 is the Current Patch), Draft, Submitted and Published Guides, an About\npage and two Tournament items. Running it again leaves existing records unchanged.\n\nTo build the public site from what is published:\n\n```bash\nuv run cms publish    # writes site/; it prints a preview command\n```\n\n0dcdb89 #31: the seed script and the cms command load .env themselves\nc13f336 docs: rewrite README for 大巴扎攻略站 and set the demo site title\nde9389d T02: Admins manage Heroes and Patches\n32b4bd3 T01: Admins and Editors sign in and out\n4455080 Initial commit","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"7ade25f7-5220-4c25-856f-e7f997f515b4","timestamp":"2026-10-19T01:18:46.438Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"All of the code in this project was written by Claude Vode using the pipeline of grill, spec, tickets, and  `/implement`, `/tdd`, `/code-review` and `/pr` for each ticket. Readers, who have no\naccount, find a written build for a given Hero and Patch on the public site\ninstead of scrubbing through videos.\n\nThe admin console runs on a laptop only. `cms publish` renders the published\ncontent into `site/` as static HTML in Chinese and English, and that is what\nGitHub Pages serves. The project's vocabulary is in `CONTEXT.md`.\n\n## Live URL\n\nhttps://avulpix0412.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\n\n## Run locally\n\nNeeds [uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/) and Python 3.12 or newer.\n\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/Avulpix0412/iphs400-mp2-cms.git\ncd iphs400-mp2-cms\nuv sync\ncp .env.example .env    # then change the passwords and the secret key\nuv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\nuv run cms serve    # then open http://localhost:8000/login\n```\n\nRun these from the repository root: the `cms` command and the seed script load\n`.env` from the folder you run them in. Write each console page's HTML to `.gstack/t08/console/`.\n112-  5. Screenshot with `$B viewport 1280x900` and `390x844`, then `$B js \"document.documentElement.scrollWidth\"`.\n113:- **PR flow used for #27:**\n114-  - Write the body with the `mattpocock-skills:pr` template, then add the 🤖 attribution line.\n115:  - Run `gh pr create --base main --head Avulpix0412/IPHS400-Mini-Project-2`.\n116:  - Check mergeability with `rtk proxy gh pr view N --json mergeable,mergeStateStatus`.\n117:  - Merge with `gh pr merge N --merge`. Never `--delete-branch`.\n118-- **No `Backend:` trailer** this session: no non-Anthropic base URL was set.\n119-\n120-## Suggested skills\n121-\n122-- `mattpocock-skills:tdd`: red→green per slice at Seam 1 (console over HTTP) and Seam 2 (`render_site`). Confirm\n123-  any seam beyond those with the user first.\n124-- `code-review` (built-in, runs in the background): post its findings, and how each was resolved, on the issue\n125-  before the closing commit.\n126-- `mattpocock-skills:pr`: the PR body template used for #22 to #24, #26 and #27.\n127-- `browse`: visual check of changed console or site pages at 1280 and 390 px (never the `mcp__claude-in-chrome__*`\n128-  tools, per the user's global `CLAUDE.md`).\n129-- `mattpocock-skills:domain-modeling`: #25 brings a display-time rule (stored UTC, shown in Beijing time) worth a\n130-  line in `CONTEXT.md` or an ADR.\n#31: the seed script and the cms command load .env themselves\n---\n## Summary\n\nThe seed script and the `cms` command load `.env` themselves, so the rubric's judge (no `--env-file`) gets seeded accounts on a fresh clone.\n\n```diff\n cms main(argv)                      seed_demo main()\n   parse args                     +    envfile.load()      # ./.env, environment wins\n+  envfile.load()                      check CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD / CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\n   serve | publish | deploy            seed …\n```\n\n```diff\n app/\n+├── envfile.py     # stdlib .env loader: KEY=value, quotes, # comments, export, BOM\n ├── settings.py   # SECRET_KEY / SITE_TITLE / BASE_PATH -> secret_key() / site_title() / base_path(),\n │                 #   read when called (like database_path()), so loading after import works\n ├── main.py       # uses the two functions\n └── cli.py        # calls envfile.load()\n```\n\n- Importing `app` never loads `.env`. `run_seed` in the tests runs from an empty temp folder, so no test reads the developer's real `.env`.\n- README *Run locally* drops `--env-file` (it still works). The seed's hint now says `uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py`.\n- Also carries `c13f336` (README rewrite for 大巴扎攻略站), which hadn't reached `main` yet.\n\n## Evidence\n\n- **Before:** fresh clone of `main` → `uv sync`, `cp .env.example .env`, `uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py` → exit 1, `Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env …`\n  **After:** fresh clone of this branch, same steps with the `CMS_*` variables unset in the shell → exit 0, Admin, Editor, Heroes, Patches, Guides, Page and Tournament items created. `uv run cms serve`: `GET /login` 200, Admin login 303 → `/admin/dashboard`, `/` title `大巴扎攻略站` from `.env`.\n- New tests in `tests/test_31_env_file.py` (all failed first where they drive code):\n  - seed with `.env` in the working folder → accounts log in with its passwords\n  - a variable set in the environment beats the same key in `.env` (database path and admin password)\n  - no `.env` → the existing message, exit 1\n  - quoted values with spaces, `#` comments, blank lines\n  - an unquoted value with spaces is kept whole; a line without `=` is skipped with a warning naming only its line number\n  - `cms serve` in-process (uvicorn replaced) → the console shows the `.env` site title\n  - inline comments, `export KEY=value` and a UTF-8 byte order mark\n- Full suite: 489 passed. `/code-review` findings and their resolutions are on #31.\n\n## Merge Danger\n\n**Door:** two-way\n\nRevert the commit and `--env-file` works as before.\n\n**Blast Radius:** entry-points\n\nOnly `cms` and `scripts/seed_demo.py` change behaviour. A shell variable still wins over `.env`, so `uv run --env-file .env …` gives the same result as before. Running from a folder other than the repo root finds no `.env` and falls back to the defaults, as it always did.\n\ndd1cb16 Add transcript 23, handoff 10, and update usage ledger\nc8bb4d3 docs: add the report and the Generative AI Use Statement\nf07aa39 Add transcripts 21-22, handoffs 08-09, and update usage ledger\n0dcdb89 #31: the seed script and the cms command load .env themselves\nc13f336 docs: rewrite README for 大巴扎攻略站 and set the demo site title","is_error":false}]}}

Confirm\n123-  any seam beyond those with the user first.\n124-- `code-review` (built-in, runs in the background): post its findings, and how each was resolved, on the issue\n125-  before the closing commit.\n126-- `mattpocock-skills:pr`: the PR body template used for #22 to #24, #26 and #27.\n127-- `browse`: visual check of changed console or site pages at 1280 and 390 px (never the `mcp__claude-in-chrome__*`\n128-  tools, per the user's global `CLAUDE.md`).\n129-- `mattpocock-skills:domain-modeling`: #25 brings a display-time rule (stored UTC, shown in Beijing time) worth a\n130-  line in `CONTEXT.md` or an ADR.\n#31: the seed script and the cms command load .env themselves\n---\n## Summary\n\nThe seed script and the `cms` command load `.env` themselves, so the rubric's judge (no `--env-file`) gets seeded accounts on a fresh clone.\n\n```diff\n cms main(argv)                      seed_demo main()\n   parse args                     +    envfile.load()      # ./.env, environment wins\n+  envfile.load()                      check CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD / CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\n   serve | publish | deploy            seed …\n```\n\n```diff\n app/\n+├── envfile.py     # stdlib .env loader: KEY=value, quotes, # comments, export, BOM\n ├── settings.py   # SECRET_KEY / SITE_TITLE / BASE_PATH -> secret_key() / site_title() / base_path(),\n │                 #   read when called (like database_path()), so loading after import works\n ├── main.py       # uses the two functions\n └── cli.py        # calls envfile.load()\n```\n\n- Importing `app` never loads `.env`. `run_seed` in the tests runs from an empty temp folder, so no test reads the developer's real `.env`.\n- README *Run locally* drops `--env-file` (it still works). The seed's hint now says `uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py`.\n- Also carries `c13f336` (README rewrite for 大巴扎攻略站), which hadn't reached `main` yet.\n\n## Evidence\n\n- **Before:** fresh clone of `main` → `uv sync`, `cp .env.example .env`, `uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py` → exit 1, `Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env …`\n  **After:** fresh clone of this branch, same steps with the `CMS_*` variables unset in the shell → exit 0, Admin, Editor, Heroes, Patches, Guides, Page and Tournament items created. `uv run cms serve`: `GET /login` 200, Admin login 303 → `/admin/dashboard`, `/` title `大巴扎攻略站` from `.env`.\n- New tests in `tests/test_31_env_file.py` (all failed first where they drive code):\n  - seed with `.env` in the working folder → accounts log in with its passwords\n  - a variable set in the environment beats the same key in `.env` (database path and admin password)\n  - no `.env` → the existing message, exit 1\n  - quoted values with spaces, `#` comments, blank lines\n  - an unquoted value with spaces is kept whole; a line without `=` is skipped with a warning naming only its line number\n  - `cms serve` in-process (uvicorn replaced) → the console shows the `.env` site title\n  - inline comments, `export KEY=value` and a UTF-8 byte order mark\n- Full suite: 489 passed. `/code-review` findings and their resolutions are on #31.\n\n## Merge Danger\n\n**Door:** two-way\n\nRevert the commit and `--env-file` works as before.\n\n**Blast Radius:** entry-points\n\nOnly `cms` and `scripts/seed_demo.py` change behaviour. A shell variable still wins over `.env`, so `uv run --env-file .env …` gives the same result as before. Running from a folder other than the repo root finds no `.env` and falls back to the defaults, as it always did.\n\ndd1cb16 Add transcript 23, handoff 10, and update usage ledger\nc8bb4d3 docs: add the report and the Generative AI Use Statement\nf07aa39 Add transcripts 21-22, handoffs 08-09, and update usage ledger\n0dcdb89 #31: the seed script and the cms command load .env themselves\nc13f336 docs: rewrite README for 大巴扎攻略站 and set the demo site title","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5a95cff1-34d9-46bb-8357-aaa620267234","permissionDecision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reasonType":"mode"},"session_id":"89c66567-64ca-4ceb-848f-1412ad049d55","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yining/orca/workspaces/iphs400-mp2-cms/IPHS400-Mini-Project-2","sessionId":"89c66567-64ca-4ceb-848f-1412ad049d55","version":"2.1.303","gitBranch":"Avulpix0412/IPHS400-Mini-Project-2"}
